	function generateUUID() {
		if ( window.crypto && typeof window.crypto.randomUUID === 'function' ) {
			return window.crypto.randomUUID();
		}
		return "xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-yxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx".replace( /[xy]/g, function( c ) {

		} catch ( e ) {
			// sessionStorage blocked.
		}
	}

	if ( window.ziffDavisAnalytics ) {

	<div class="grid">
		<div class="single-page__content">
			
<div class="entry-content" data-autopogo>
	<figure class="wp-block-image size-large"><img fetchpriority="high" decoding="async" width="1000" height="500" src="https://www.zdnet.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/df848f27-9c32-4c78-8e1f-5e513f752bd9.jpg?resize=1200,675" alt="EU flag" class="wp-image-1177132" srcset="https://www.zdnet.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/df848f27-9c32-4c78-8e1f-5e513f752bd9.jpg 1000w, https://www.zdnet.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/df848f27-9c32-4c78-8e1f-5e513f752bd9.jpg?resize=300,150 300w, https://www.zdnet.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/df848f27-9c32-4c78-8e1f-5e513f752bd9.jpg?resize=768,384 768w" sizes="(max-width: 1000px) 100vw, 1000px" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption"><p></p><span class="zd-image-credit">Image: Guillaume Périgois</span></figcaption></figure>


<p>The European Union has imposed <a href="https://eur-lex.europa.eu/legal-content/EN/TXT/?uri=uriserv%3AOJ.LI.2020.351.01.0001.01.ENG" target="_blank" rel="noopener">sanctions</a> today against Russia for its involvement in the 2015 German Parliament (Bundestag) hack.</p>



<p>Sanctions were levied against the GRU (Russian Main Intelligence Directorate), a military intelligence agency part of the Russian Army, and two of its officers.</p>



<p>The two GRU officers were identified as Dmitry Badin and Igor Kostyukov.</p>

<div class="wp-block-ziff-davis-exco-video"
	id="exco-video-block-6a8e06fe4d2cfb09fd68fb54-1177154-1"
	data-exco-video-id=""
	data-exco-playlist-id="6a8e06fe4d2cfb09fd68fb54"
	data-exco-video-title=""
	data-exco-video-src=""
	data-exco-video-duration="0"
	data-exco-video-poster=""
	data-exco-suppress-ads="0"
	data-exco-autoplay="0"
	data-exco-sticky="1">
	<div class="exco-video-player" id="exco-playlist-6a8e06fe4d2cfb09fd68fb54-1177154-1">
			</div>
</div>




<p>EU officials said Badin was part of a team of Russian military intelligence officers who hacked the Bundestag IT network between April and May 2015.</p>



<p>&#8220;This cyber-attack targeted the parliament&#8217;s information system and affected its operation for several days,&#8221; the EU said today. &#8220;A significant amount of data was stolen and the email accounts of several MPs as well as of Chancellor Angela Merkel were affected.&#8221;</p>



<p>Kostyukov was sanctioned for his role as First Deputy Head of the GRU.</p>



<p>EU officials said Kostyukov commands the 85th Main Centre for Special Services (GTsSS), also known as Military Unit 26165, but more commonly known in the cyber-security industry under the hacker codenames of <a href="https://malpedia.caad.fkie.fraunhofer.de/actor/sofacy" target="_blank" rel="noopener">APT28, Fancy Bear, Sofacy, or Strontium</a>.</p>



<p>German authorities have been pushing for official EU sanctions against Russia for the 2015 hack since earlier this year when they <a href="https://www.zdnet.com/article/german-authorities-charge-russian-hacker-for-2015-bundestag-hack/" target="_blank">filed official charges against Badin</a>.</p>



<p><a href="https://www.zdnet.com/article/russia-says-germany-has-not-provided-any-evidence-of-bundestag-hack/" target="_blank">Russian authorities said Germany never provided any evidence</a> in regards to the 2015 Bundestag hack and the Badin charges, accusing the Berlin government of chasing sanctions rather than actually wanting to get the GRU officer in a court of law.</p>



<p>Badin was <a href="https://www.justice.gov/opa/pr/us-charges-russian-gru-officers-international-hacking-and-related-influence-and" target="_blank" rel="noopener">also charged in the US</a> for a long string of hacks while part of APT28, such as cyber-attacks against the World Anti-Doping Agency&#8217;s (WADA), the Organisation for the Prohibition of Chemical Weapons (OPCW), and involvement in US political disinformation efforts.</p>



<p>Today&#8217;s announcement is the second wave of sanctions imposed against Russian hackers by the EU this year.</p>



<p>Brussels officials <a href="https://www.zdnet.com/article/eu-sanctions-china-russia-and-north-korea-for-past-hacks/" target="_blank">sanctioned four GRU officers</a> at the end of July for the attempted hack of the OPCW WiFi network. Sanctions were also levied against Chinese and North Korean hackers as well.</p>



<p>The sanctions consist of a travel ban and an asset freeze. EU citizens and businesses are prohibited from engaging in transactions with any sanctioned entities.</p>



<div class="zd-gallery">
	
<div class="zd-gallery__wrapper zd-gallery__wrapper--grid">
	<h2>The world&#8217;s most famous and dangerous APT (state-developed) malware</h2>

	<div class="zd-gallery__grid-items">

<script type="text/javascript" data-script-id="admiral">
( function() {
	var targeting = {"pageType":"single","topic":"Security,EU","siteHier":"Tech,Security"};
	var scriptUrl = "https://skullmagnets.com/public/js/7c30141/c0_f76c0a89e968f71ca4bb062649d.bundle.js";
	var storageKey = "_aQS02MzI0RDgwRjcxOTJEQUM5NjJDQUMwN0UtMQ";

	function loadAdmiral() {
		// Inject the bootstrap once per page lifecycle.
		if ( window.__zdAdmiralLoaded ) {
			return;
		}
		window.__zdAdmiralLoaded = true;

		// 1. Queueing function available before the vendor bundle loads.
		window.admiral = window.admiral || function() {
			( admiral.q = admiral.q || [] ).push( arguments );
		};

		// 2. Page targeting from server-computed metadata.
		window.admiral( 'targeting', 'pause', 3000 );
		window.admiral( 'targeting', 'set', 'pageType', targeting.pageType || '' );
		window.admiral( 'targeting', 'set', 'topic', targeting.topic || '' );
		window.admiral( 'targeting', 'set', 'siteHier', targeting.siteHier || '' );
		window.admiral( 'targeting', 'ready' );

		// 3. Forward Admiral engagement state from localStorage into GPT targeting.
		( function( windowRef, admiralName ) {
			// This is the canonical Admiral snippet, kept verbatim from legacy CNET.
			// The queue function is already defined in step 1, so this `||` guard is a
			// no-op here; only the `.v = 2` version stamp below is doing new work. The
			// snippet is preserved intact rather than trimmed so it stays diff-clean
			// against upstream Admiral.
			windowRef[ admiralName ] = windowRef[ admiralName ] || function queueAdmiral() {
				( queueAdmiral.q = queueAdmiral.q || [] ).push( arguments );
			};
			windowRef[ admiralName ].v = windowRef[ admiralName ].v || 2;

			function getAdmiralTargeting() {
				try {
					// storageKey is per Admiral property; see the feature config.
					var stored = storageKey ? windowRef.localStorage.getItem( storageKey ) : null;
					if ( stored ) {
						return JSON.parse( stored ).lgk || [];
					}

					var legacy = windowRef.localStorage.getItem( '%764%61%631e%69Z%720' ) || '';
					if ( legacy.split( ',' )[4] > 0 ) {
						return [ [ admiralName + '-engaged', 'true' ] ];
					}
				} catch ( error ) {}
			}

			function applyGptTargeting() {
				var values = getAdmiralTargeting();
				var pubads = windowRef.googletag && windowRef.googletag.pubads && windowRef.googletag.pubads();

				if ( values && typeof values.forEach === 'function' && pubads ) {
					values.forEach( function( pair ) {
						if ( pair && pair[0] ) {
							pubads.setTargeting( pair[0], pair[1] || '' );
						}
					} );
				}
			}

			windowRef.googletag = windowRef.googletag || {};
			windowRef.googletag.cmd = windowRef.googletag.cmd || [];

			if ( typeof windowRef.googletag.pubads === 'function' ) {
				applyGptTargeting();
			} else if ( typeof windowRef.googletag.cmd.unshift === 'function' ) {
				windowRef.googletag.cmd.unshift( applyGptTargeting );
			} else {
				windowRef.googletag.cmd.push( applyGptTargeting );
			}
		} )( window, 'admiral' );

		// 4. Append the external Admiral vendor bundle asynchronously.
		( function( documentRef, src ) {
			var externalScript = documentRef.createElement( 'script' );
			var firstScript = documentRef.getElementsByTagName( 'script' )[0];
			externalScript.async = true;
			externalScript.src = src;
			firstScript.parentNode.insertBefore( externalScript, firstScript );
		} )( document, scriptUrl );
	}

	// Wait for DOMContentLoaded before injecting, matching legacy CNET timing.
	function whenReady() {
		if ( document.readyState === 'loading' ) {
			document.addEventListener( 'DOMContentLoaded', loadAdmiral, { once: true } );
		} else {
			loadAdmiral();
		}
	}

	// Route through the zdconsent third-party-script consent gate. Fail-closed:
	// if the gate is absent (consent feature disabled/unconfigured, or zdconsent
	// has not initialized), do NOT boot — Admiral is a third-party tracker and
	// must not load without consent. Matches the push-notifications convention.
	if ( window.zdconsent?.run?.push ) {
		window.zdconsent.run.push( whenReady );
	}
} )();
</script>
